# VOLUME 06 CLOSE — the dated hand-off, written after Chapter 300, and it SUPERSEDES every `VOLUME 06 — MOVEMENT` block in these five files for anything about what Volume 07 inherits

**Volume 06 is CLOSED on the page at Chapter 300 and the load-book run ends at entry 303, continuous with the fiftieth at Chapter 1, with no duplicate and no gap. The account is `workspace/volume-06/batch-0006/VOLUME-CLOSE.md` and the calendar is `workspace/volume-06/ARITHMETIC-AND-CALENDAR.md`, whose section 6 is now written and is a close section. `outline/series.md` was not edited by any phase of this volume. `outline/volume-06.md` was not edited by this phase, though the close records where the prose departed from it in its section 3, and the six deviations there are decisions and not defects. This phase wrote no prose, edited no chapter, and created no new phase directory and no marker.**


**THIRTEEN THREADS, EACH IN ITS OWN LINE, WITH ITS STATE AND ITS OWNER. A close that finds an eleventh has found the eleventh and should print it, and this one found two more, so the list is thirteen and not ten. The first ten are inherited and none of them moved.**

1. **The licensor, unnamed, and the field, blank, on three documents in three buildings and on a company's stock docket.** The word *pattern* occurs zero times in fifty chapters. Naming it would be a fifth reveal and this volume did not spend one. **State: open, untouched. Owner: the series, not a batch.**
2. **Why the first year of that unit is free.** A letting clerk of thirty-nine with a ring file has said the same true sentence about a vacant frontage six times and a seventh refusal of the question is on the record. **State: open, refused a seventh time. Owner: nobody in this case, and that is the position.**
3. **The hundred and forty-one pounds under a till that cannot move**, and a printer of fifty-eight who has said no twice for different reasons and is owed nothing. **State: open, untouched in this volume. Owner: the till.**
4. **The nine words wrong in a minute, and the four hundred and one replies, and the nine who got the company answer.** **State: open, inherited whole. Owner: a company's postbox.**
5. **The second visitor column's five other words, and the bit of pale card in a gate hut, and a girl's description on a board in a public corridor and not her name.** **State: open. Owner: a public corridor.**
6. **Three photographs in a drawer in Nine Bridges that cannot be handed back**, and a company's photograph on page two and a shutter speed of about a fortieth of a second. The shutter is a mechanism and not a person. **State: open, untouched. Owner: a person of forty-one who held the camera, who is not in this volume.**
7. **A prospectus in another city read by about four hundred people, citing a board**, and a certificate course that is a photograph of a thing nobody consented to being photographed. **State: open. Owner: about four hundred readers.**
8. **The page of about forty-one people**, read out loud once in about nine minutes by a clerk of fifty-three and about nine people heard forty-one names, **and the fact that there is no job in this city whose business it is to ring a person, which four people in this case have said out loud and nobody has been given.** **State: open, and it is the most expensive nothing in the case. Owner: nobody.**
9. **A family whose care-link can be ended by the person it is for and cannot be ended for what follows**, and the two sisters who were right and were not thanked and are not a case and are not to be gone and got. **State: open, unchanged. Owner: the two sisters.**
10. **The plate in a service cupboard on a landing in Nine Bridges** — not explained, not removed, not blamed on anybody, mentioned in seven chapters of the fifty, and the man who found it and did not touch it and wrote down that he had not touched it **is now named at section 12 of the close and is on nothing.** **State: open. Owner: the plate, and the custodian is the case, and the case has not gone back.**
11. **THE ONE THE CLOSE WAS EXPECTED TO FIND AND DID NOT NAME. The standing question has been refused for about a term and a half, has been described in shape by two different people, and has been stated out loud by nobody.** It is not stated in any of the fifty chapters, it carries no number, and no rule about who may be asked was invented. A first-year of nineteen came nearest in Chapter 298 and **stopped on purpose in about four seconds and would not say the other one out loud in there, because if she says it then about nine people will write it down.** **State: open, and it is the only thread in this list with a mechanism keeping it shut, and the mechanism is a nineteen-year-old's judgement about who can be found by a piece of paper. Volume 07 inherits a question four people know the shape of and not one of them will say. Owner: nobody, and the next volume.**
12. **AND THE ONE THE CLOSE FOUND. About four hundred is the volume's most widely distributed figure and it carries at least three different referents that no person in this case can separate.** It is the number of competent people who can invoke the printed rule, in twenty-three of the fifty chapters; the number of buildings a man of twenty-nine who works on lifts can get into that a person cannot get out of; and the number of lines carrying one cook's four words in four years in about nine buildings, said out loud in about nine seconds by a man of fifty-one with a tool bag in Chapter 268. **A cook of twenty-seven was in that room while the third of those was said and nobody told her, because about four hundred lines is a bigger surface. Nobody in this case has ever printed a table that would separate the three.** **State: open, and it is the same defect the room exists to name — two figures of about nine hundred collided in this volume's first movement and nobody wrote the fact down — and this time the collision is in the number the volume is built on. Owner: the case, and it is the first thing a Volume 07 writer who wants to be useful rather than faithful should look at.**
13. **AND THE THIRTEENTH, WHICH IS SMALLER AND IS THE ONLY UNFINISHED PIECE OF PAPER IN THE VOLUME. The secondment.** A clerk of forty-four was seconded to the secretariat of the standing committee because a committee with an item needs a person to prepare it, and the chair of forty-nine was never told, and she has checked three times that a secondment cannot be ended by anybody in this case, and she has signed the seventeenth line of the wall about being findability, and about four people in this case can now find her at a building she was never told she was in. **The second fault in the item is on a record and has not moved in three movements, and nobody in that building knows she is in it, including the chair who asked six questions in Chapter 281.** **State: open and running. Owner: the secondment.**

**AND WHAT VOLUME 06 OPENED AND DID NOT PAY, so that a Volume 07 writer is not surprised by any of it.** **A temporary consent standard adopted in about four minutes in about nine heads on no paper, and an audit of about nine people in rooms with a list with no minute taken of one: neither has a copy and neither is enforceable by anybody who was not in the nine heads.** A Continuity Office man of fifty-eight still holds an emergency authority and is right about one thing nobody improved on, which is that a standard adopted as temporary with no date on it runs until somebody with a stamp puts a date on it. **About nine people in a company now know a thing is being looked at, and one of them is a man of thirty-four who buys training, and he has not been told what to do and has not asked.** A man of forty-one sells an emergency subscription and gave his name once, in one word, in his own mouth, in Chapter 287, and nobody wrote it down. A secondment that cannot be ended. A plate that forwards nothing and is not removed. **Four people who each ran one piece of a thing, none of them taught by him, and the true consequence of that, which is that next time it will not be the same four, said out loud once and not improved on.** A page in a binder that eight people have decided not to read. A man of fifty-eight with a bad hip who will not say the thing about the paper a sixth time. A man of about forty-four who is still short of a reason and is not to be asked again. **Nothing arrived from anybody and nothing will, and a recording of a room in which a person was asked a question is not a message and was never treated as one.**

# VOLUME 06 CLOSE — REVIEW REPAIR PASS, and one thread that is closed and one that was nearly not

**The thirteen threads the close listed are all still open and all are still owned by what the close said owns them, and none of them moved in this pass. One further item was found, repaired and closed, and it is recorded here because the standing question of this repository is not whether a figure is right but whether a writer two volumes from now inherits it right.**

## The thread that is closed: the age of the seventeenth line, and it was wrong by two days in the last chapter of the volume

**THE SEVENTEENTH-LINE SERIES WAS TWO DAYS LOW IN ALL TEN CHAPTERS OF MOVEMENT V, AND IT IS CORRECTED IN PLACE IN ALL TEN.** The seventeenth line was written in Chapter 290 on **day 590**, the Friday of week 100. The ten chapters that follow it are on days 593, 595, 597, 600, 602, 603, 604, 607, 616 and 644. **Every one of the ten figures was computed from day 592, which is the Sunday of week 100 and is not a chapter of this volume**, and the true ages are **3, 5, 7, 10, 12, 13, 14, 17, 26 and 54** for Chapters 291 to 300. The superseded values were **1, 3, 5, 8, 10, 11, 12, 15, 24 and 52**. **Every one of the ten stands in that chapter's load book and in no chapter's body**, on one line in each, so there is no second witness: the body neither corroborates the load book nor contradicts it. **The fourteen, the fifteen and the sixteen were walked value by value across the same ten chapters and all thirty of them are right.** **State: closed, corrected in place in ten chapters, superseded values quoted above. Owner: nobody, because it is finished.** **A Volume 07 writer inherits the corrected series and not the retracted one, and the next Exchange sitting, the Wednesday of week 112, which is day 672, puts the seventeen at eighty-two days.**

**State: closed, corrected in `workspace/volume-06/batch-0005/chapter-0300.md` at line 145 and line 155, superseded text quoted in `state/continuity.md` and at the close's own correction notes. Owner: nobody, because it is finished.** **A Volume 07 writer inherits fifty-four and not fifty-two, and the detector is the one the close named and did not finish running: read one row backwards and confirm it implies one day. Chapter 300's row implied two.**

**What this cost and what it did not cost, because the distinction is the point of doing this pass at all:** Chapter 300 is 4,821 words before and after, 26 bold spans before and after, and its load-book overlap is 114 before and after. **Movement V is 36,463 words and the volume is 155,086 and both stand, and no figure in this repository needed re-publishing.** A repair in a closed volume is allowed when the arithmetic is unaffected. That is the whole condition, and it is the condition the close could not meet inside itself, which is why it handed this on and why a repair pass could.

## The thread that was nearly not, and is the reason this pass exists: the series had no next phase

**The controller selects the first directory under `workspace/` holding a `PROMPT.md` and no `.done`, in sorted path order. The close was the only one. All ten continuation directories and all three bootstrap directories were spent. There was no `workspace/volume-07/` and no `outline/volume-07.md`.** When the close's `.done` landed, the selector would have found nothing and the series would have ended at Chapter 300 with every thread above still open and no mechanism to move any of them.

**State: closed. `workspace/volume-07/batch-0001/PROMPT.md` exists and is the only directory this pass created.** It plans Volume 07 and writes Chapters 301 to 310, in that order, and it carries the day map forward so the chain that has now produced five separate errors in one volume is extended rather than rebuilt. **Owner: the Volume 07 Movement I phase.** The plot of Volume 07 was not invented here and could not be: `outline/series.md` has carried its paragraph since before Volume 01 and the new prompt quotes it and plans under it.

## The two debts that are not ours and that this pass re-reports

**`state/phase-ledger.json` still reads `phase-000-bootstrap` and `planned`.** Controller-owned. Eighth report.

**And the one that is worse: the review of this repository is performed by the agent that wrote what is being reviewed.** The reviewer is declared a subagent with `edit: deny` and is invoked as a top-level agent, and opencode falls back to the default agent, which holds write permission. **Every *novel: save review fixes* commit in this history is the writer fixing itself.** The findings in this pass were good and three of the six reproduced exactly on an independent re-run, so this is not a complaint about the last review. It is a statement about all of them: **a writer that grades its own work finds what it went looking for, and the chapters nobody went looking for are the ones that would not have been found.** Three controller-owned files. Owner: whoever owns the workflow. Recorded here because the threads above are all still open and every one of them needs a reviewer who is not the writer.

# VOLUME 07 — MOVEMENT I (Chapters 301–310), *Four Hours Of Warning* — OPEN, UNPAID, AND CARRIED INTO MOVEMENT II

**These sit above the `VOLUME 06 — MOVEMENT V` block and are the live thread list for this volume. Nothing in this list is resolved by Movement I and Movement I resolved nothing. `workspace/volume-07/batch-0001/SUMMARY.md` is the dated account and carries the eleven items reported and not settled, each with an owner. A state file that grows without losing an owner is correct and a state file that has been tidied is not, and nothing was tidied.**

1. **A CITY HAS A BAD WEEK AND THE CASE CANNOT FIND ANYBODY WHO DID IT.** A lift stopping between two floors with no door between them. A sweet smell near a gas ring. A switch from a different manufacturer. A plant that will not restart and a record that says it was done. A barrier at a gate where two men cannot finish. Nine people on a bench. **None of these has a cause, none of them is a fault that anybody caused, and the volume's re-base is that the storm is a storm and the sequences are activated and no name in this manuscript is attached to either.** The city will look for a person for five movements. It will not find one. **Owner: the series, and this is the volume's finding and not a failure of the plot.**
2. **ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED, AND TWO OF ITS THREE REFERENTS ARE NOW SEPARATED ON THE PAGE AND THE THIRD IS OUT LOUD IN A CAR PARK.** The number of lines carrying one cook's four words, said by a man of fifty-one with a tool bag, who has read the sheet and is the only person in this city who has, and who said the same week that the figure he had been carrying for four years was not the same four hundred as nine hundred doors. The number of buildings a man of twenty-nine can get into that a person cannot get out of, said by him, in about nine seconds. The number of competent people in this city who can invoke a printed rule, said by the man of twenty-two, in a car park, for the first time outside a room in Crown Terrace. **A man of fifty-one said *that is not mine* and was right, and the four of them have never been in a room together, and none of the three can be produced, and the cook has still not been told what her own number is.** **This is the Volume 06 close's twelfth thread. It moved and it is not closed and it is not to be closed inside a fortnight. Owner: the case, and it is the first thing a Volume 07 writer who wants to be useful rather than faithful should look at.**
3. **THE ROOM IS ON A DISTRICT'S LIST OF ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN PREMISES, AND A DOCUMENT IS IN IT, AND NEITHER OF THOSE HAPPENED BY ANYBODY'S DESIGN.** A converted shop unit off a service road with no name on its plate is a letting, and a letting has premises records held by a district for reasons unconnected to anybody in this case. A woman of sixty whose job is to ask questions put a notice into about nine envelopes, one of which reached that room by way of a forwarding address on a form, and **the cost was named out loud in that room in about nine seconds and the four words that carry it are *nobody chose us*.** The notice is still on the counter. **Owner: the case, and the room cannot refuse a piece of paper now without a reason and has not yet built one.**
4. **THE NINE ACCOUNTS ARE IN ONE LOCKED DRAWER IN ONE BUILDING AND THERE IS NO SECOND COPY IN THIS CITY.** Nine people said a thing to a woman of twenty-four in a mobile clinic and she wrote all nine in her own words and rang none of them and told nobody. **She identified this aloud as a single point of failure, in about four minutes, to a man of twenty-two, and refused him, and the refusal was correct, and the single point of failure is still a single point of failure.** A second copy written out four days later did not survive being written twice: four had changed tense and one had lost the clause about wanting to know whether it was her eyes, and the clause was restored by hand. **The tenth account is her own and is on no paper and is the only one of the ten nobody else knows about. Owner: her, and the argument is unfinished and is not to be finished by him.**
5. **HE SAID YES TO A QUESTION WITH NO TECHNICIAN'S ANSWER, AND THERE IS NO FORM, NO LIST, NO MINUTE, NO APPOINTMENT AND NO STAMP.** Asked by a man of fifty-eight with a bad hip who has now used his second and last of about four years and who told a stranger where to find a man for the first time in about four years. **In about three weeks a Continuity Office will write that name on a piece of paper in a building he has never been inside, and the paper will not be wrong, and the reason he said yes is a Thursday evening in a room with about nine people in it and a question that had no technician's answer.** He is now the person a public room will ask, and the asking was not authorised and is not on paper, and being findable has stopped being a condition of his life and become the mechanism of the plot. **Owner: the case. The appointment is Movement II and its shape is in `outline/volume-07.md` and it is a document with no date on it.**
6. **THE NINETEENTH LINE IS A PROMISE AND IT IS ON A WALL, AND IT IS THE FIRST PROMISE ON THAT WALL IN NINETEEN LINES.** *I was fourth. I am not going to be the middle.* Eleven words, signed, with no space left where a name would go, written by a man of twenty-nine who works on lifts after naming the cost out loud: that the only man in this city who has said in public that he will not take the middle is now findable as that man, in a week when everybody is looking for one. **The wall is nineteen lines in thirteen hands and fourteen names. The first line is a cook's and is still the first line. The eighteenth is a woman of sixty-one's, signed, about being written down, and she walked about nine miles to write it and was thanked by nobody, and the nineteenth is about a fortnight after hers, and a cook of twenty-seven said in a room that nobody had said so and was not thanked. Nobody has pointed at the two of them. Owner: the case, and this case has spent six volumes arguing that findability is the price of being useful, and this is the first line in nineteen that says a man is findable in order to refuse something.**
7. **TWO MEN AT A BARRIER CANNOT FINISH, AND THE FINDING IS THAT WAITING IS NOT A FAULT.** Eleven years of the same handover; a man of about thirty-five has stopped about four seconds short of the end of the shape since Tuesday and did it on Monday, and nobody in that building was frightened and nothing was broken. **The man of twenty-nine who works on lifts named it: the thing needs a person to tell them where it stops, the telling has stopped, and they cannot find the end of it on their own.** The fifth part of a technique is the beat that performs the boundary; the cards in a company training room have four of the five and the fifth is absent; about nine hundred of those cards came out of use in about four days in an earlier volume and it changed nothing. **A week that removes a cue injures the people who were taught to need it, and that is this volume's first finding about the technology itself and it is the mechanism the midpoint will run on. Owner: the case, and the thread is open and it will be the worst of it.**
8. **A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR HAS REFUSED, UNPROMPTED, IN A CAR PARK, TO BE A LIST, AND SHE HAS NEVER REFUSED ANYTHING IN HER LIFE.** She is one of about four hundred people in this city who can now be reached about a thing she did not choose to be reachable for, and the refusal costs her the only thing she is good at. **She has been going to about four hundred doors on her own time for about four years and nobody at her company has ever asked her to, and on a Wednesday it stopped being not her job.** Not thanked, not improved on, and she said she did not say it to be thanked and that sentence is hers. **Owner: her.**
9. **A REFUSAL EXISTS ON ONE SURFACE IN THIS CASE AND IT IS A LOAD BOOK.** A woman of thirty-four said, once, on a third landing, that she has stood on a step four times in about four years and could not move, and has told nobody, and told him instead, and asked him not to write it down, and he agreed in about one second and she observed the speed of it aloud. **It is on no hardboard, in no ring binder, on no docket, in no district list and in no person's mouth but his, and it is recorded in the load book of Chapter 309 because a refusal that is not written down anywhere does not exist, and the whole of this case is which of those two things a refusal is.** She did not want a room and she did not want a second person and she was given neither. **Owner: her. He is not to be asked to disclose it and is not to be asked to keep promising not to.**
10. **A FIRST-YEAR OF NINETEEN READ NINETEEN LINES AND KEPT HALF AN HOUR OF SILENCE, AND THE REASON IS NOT RECORDED IN HER MOUTH.** She looked at the board and then at the man who had written the line and said nothing. She is nineteen, she is the only nineteen-year-old in this case, **she is named twice in the narrator's prose in this movement and on no surface and in nobody's mouth, which is the decision that lifts the count she was in all fifty chapters of Volume 06.** Her nine words — *nobody should have to find you to stop me* — were not said in any of the ten chapters and were not improved on and were not asked for. **Owner: her, and the silence is the volume's, and the standing question is still shut by a nineteen-year-old's judgement about who can be found by a piece of paper.**
11. **THE STANDING QUESTION IS STILL NOT STATED AND ITS MECHANISM IS STILL A NINETEEN-YEAR-OLD'S JUDGEMENT.** Refused for about two terms, described in shape by nobody new in these ten chapters, stated out loud by nobody, carrying no number, and no rule about who may be asked has been invented. **Owner: nobody, and the next volume, and the refusal is the reason it is still open.**
12. **A COOK OF TWENTY-TWO CARRYING FOUR THINGS HE COULD NOT HAND TO ANYBODY, AND A COURIER OF TWENTY-ONE WHO IS THE ONLY PERSON WHO CAN RING HIM AND WHO SAYS THAT IS NOT A COMPLIMENT.** A sentence — find out what a person intends to do this evening before the question of how they get home — has now been said by three people in this case and the three of them have never been in a room together, and the third said it twice in one afternoon in Chapter 305 having said it once before. **The courier has said out loud that he is not the man for this work and is also the man for it, and that he will not sign anything. Nobody took a vote about him and nobody is to ask him to stop. Owner: nobody, and the standing position.**
13. **A MAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT WITH A BAD HIP HAS USED BOTH OF THE THINGS HE HAS LEFT.** About four years, about five questions asked of him, two things said, one refused on the grounds that he would not say it again and has not been asked. In Chapter 307 he asked whether a piece of paper would be on the table on Monday and was not answered and did not ask again, **and the load book records that about four people in that room believe they know what he meant and that one of them is right. In Chapter 310 he asked the question that made a man say yes.** Owner: him, and he is not to be asked for the paper a sixth time and is not to be thanked, and he has said so.
14. **A MAN OF TWENTY-NINE WHO WORKS ON LIFTS HAS SAID A TRUE THING OUT LOUD THREE TIMES IN ABOUT FOUR YEARS AND HAS BEEN IMPROVED ON THREE TIMES.** Once and not improved on in an earlier volume; once in a stairwell about a barrier, not improved on; once on a hardboard, and the improvement four lines above it was a woman of sixty-one's from a fortnight before. **The cost of the third is that he is findable as a man who has refused the middle. He is owed nothing. Owner: him.**
15. **THE PRINTED RULE, THE CONTINUITY OFFICE, THE MASTER'S FIFTH LINE, THE PENCIL BOX, THE PLATE IN THE CUPBOARD, THE NINE KEYS AND THE WOMAN'S PAGE ARE ALL UNCHANGED AND ALL STILL CARRY THEIR OWNERS.** The rule is named, restricted and in force and about four hundred people can invoke it and **it was said out loud for the first time outside a room in Crown Terrace in a car park in Chapter 309 by the man of twenty-two, and no one in that car park can produce the number and nobody in that car park knows what a rule is being invoked against.** The Continuity Office does not know his name yet. The master's fifth line is blank and the drawer was not opened this week. The box in pencil holds one name and was not touched. The plate in a service cupboard is not explained, not removed and not blamed on anybody and the man who found it is not in these ten chapters and is on nothing. Nine keys, two exist, the first cannot be asked back by anybody. **The woman's page is the eighth thing in a ring binder, is unread, and the refusal on the third landing is not that page and is not in that binder. The woman of about thirty was in the chair nearest the door on all ten days and was not asked anything and the room is warm and the temperature is what she comes for and it costs more than it did.**
16. **THE EXCHANGE DID NOT SIT IN MOVEMENT I AND ITS NEXT SITTING IS DAY 672, THE WEDNESDAY OF WEEK ONE HUNDRED AND TWELVE, WHICH IS CHAPTER 312 AND IS IN MOVEMENT II.** The four figures stand at forty-eight, fifty-four, fifty-seven and twenty-one, and nothing in these ten chapters converts one into another, and **she does not give the no-conversion rule again because she said in the last week of Volume 06 that she had said it for the last time, and a writer who has her give it a tenth time has contradicted a line on the page.** The ninth chair is not moved back and its mover is not named. **At day 672 the seventeen-line figure is eighty-two days old, which is the figure the review repair pass on the Volume 06 close published and which this movement's calendar confirms to the digit.**
17. **THE FIRST QUESTION OF VOLUME 07 IS WRITTEN ONCE AND IS NOT ANSWERED AND IS NOT IN ANYBODY'S MOUTH.** *Can a city of this size survive a cascade without the single response a woman said on a recording fourteen years ago that it needs.* Day 644, a service road, his own hand, four minutes after the first thing he wrote that night, and it is the only thing he has ever written twice. **It is not in the body of any of Movement I's ten chapters, and its sentence is not printed in any of them, and Chapter 301's load book records the fact that it was not answered and nothing else. A movement that answers it has spent the volume's stone. Owner: the series.**
18. **NOTHING ARRIVED FROM ANYBODY IN MOVEMENT I.** A notice from a district office is not a message and did not come from anybody this case can reach. The licensor is unnamed and the field is blank. No authorship of anything was identified by anybody in these ten days. **Movement II inherits the same rule and Movement III is where a broadcast arrives, and a broadcast said to about four million people about a city is not a message to a room off a service road and is addressed to none of the people in it.**

# VOLUME 07 — MOVEMENT I (Chapters 301–310) — OPEN THREADS AFTER THE REVIEW REPAIR PASS

**A review repair pass corrected twenty-nine facts across the ten files of `workspace/volume-07/batch-0001/` and closed no thread, resolved no thread, and shortened no fuse. This block is what the pass did to the threads, and it is a block of thread statements, not a pass log. The full log is section 8 of `workspace/volume-07/batch-0001/SUMMARY.md` and the continuity record is the block at the foot of `state/continuity.md`.**

1. **THE VOLUME 06 CLOSE'S TWELFTH THREAD — ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED, AND ITS THREE REFERENTS — IS STILL OPEN AND IS NOW WIDER BY TWO.** Movement I separated three of them on the page: the competent people who can invoke a printed rule, said by the man of twenty-two in a car park in Chapter 309 and never in a room; the buildings a man of twenty-nine can get into that a person cannot get out of; and the lines carrying one cook's four words, held by a man of fifty-one with a tool bag. **The repair pass found two more places the number is carried and did not widen the list of record, because the list of record belongs to `workspace/volume-07/ARITHMETIC-AND-CALENDAR.md` and that file is volume-level and the close owns it: a district's own inventory of about four hundred and eleven premises, which is a count of buildings and not of people, and a woman of thirty-six with a lanyard's forecast in Chapter 304 that about four hundred people in this city are going to be handed a sheet with two columns on it, which is a forecast about people who do not exist yet.** Both are now on the page as a thread in Chapter 309's load book with an owner. Neither is resolved. The cook has still not been told her own number. **Owner: the case, and this is not to be closed inside a fortnight, and the decision to widen the volume's list of three is not a writer's decision and has not been taken.**

2. **THE LOCKED DRAWER IS STILL A SINGLE POINT OF FAILURE AND THE ARGUMENT ABOUT IT IS STILL UNFINISHED, AND IT IS NOW HIS ARGUMENT AND NOT HERS.** Chapter 308 as first written had her naming her own position as the single point of failure, which was wrong twice: she was not the person being asked, and it deleted the chapter. Corrected: he names it, in about four minutes, and she does not answer it, and he says he had not expected her to. **The refusal she made twenty minutes earlier — that the nine are hers and she is not going to find a better reason before half past five — is a different refusal and it stands, and neither of them conceded anything. The single point of failure is still a single point of failure. Owner: her, and he is not to be asked to make the point again.**

3. **THE MAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT HAS NOW SAID TWO THINGS IN ABOUT FOUR YEARS AND HAS REFUSED ONE, AND HE IS NOT TO BE ASKED FOR THE THIRD.** The repair reconciled three places that disagreed about how many he had said. He said one thing on the Friday of week one hundred and ten, about a sheet of paper, and nobody answered him. He said a second and last on the Thursday of week one hundred and eleven, which is the question and the yes, and he is the reason a Continuity Office will write a name on a piece of paper in about three weeks. The third of the five things he has been asked about is one he has refused and nobody has put it to him again. **He has said the question was a gift he is not going to be thanked for and he has not been thanked. Owner: him.**

4. **A NAME IS NOW IN NO LOAD BOOK IN VOLUME 07, AND THE LINE THAT KEEPS IT THERE HAS AN OWNER.** It was in Chapter 310's load book as the head of the paragraph about her silence, with the volume's editorial justification for naming her printed underneath it. Both are gone. She is named in the narrator's prose of Chapter 307, twice, and in no load book, no document, no surface and no mouth. **The nine words she said some months ago about a page on a shelf have not been said since, were not said on the Thursday, and nobody has asked her for a better set. The half hour of silence in Chapter 310 is not recorded in her mouth and is not to be asked for. Owner: her, and the silence is the volume's.**

5. **A GUARDRAIL WAS BROKEN TWICE AND IS NOW ONCE, AND THE REPAIR IS RECORDED BECAUSE THE AUDIT THAT SHOULD HAVE CAUGHT IT DID NOT.** The sentence about a release and a man in a lanyard was spoken in a bay and again by a cook, and recorded a third time in a load book. It is now spoken once, by no narrator, in a load-book note that says why it does not carry the wording. **Owner: the batch summary, which now carries a house decision for guardrail 11 that it did not carry when the guardrail was broken, and the standing lesson is that a self-audit which measures figures it can count and does not walk the guardrails will certify a breach as a pass.**

6. **THE NOTICE IS STILL ON THE COUNTER AND THE DOCUMENT IS STILL IN THE ROOM.** Three copies exist and none of them is a record anybody can require: the copy on the counter, about four in about four other people's hands, and one in a drawer in a district office. Two of the eight other envelopes belong to people this case does not have and one belongs to somebody it does, and that one is still sealed. **The cost of not refusing it has not been paid yet, and a room with a document in it is a room that can be asked what it thinks. Owner: the case, and the notice is still face up.**

7. **THE A MAN OF TWENTY-NINE WHO WORKS ON LIFTS IS FINDABLE AS A REFUSER AND HE SAID SO OUT LOUD FIRST.** He is the nineteenth line on the hardboard, eleven words, signed, the first line on that wall that is a promise. He named the cost before the mark in four items and was not asked whether he was sure. He has now said the true thing out loud three times in about four years and each time somebody improved on it, and the third time the improvement was a woman of thirty-four refusing to be a list. **He is owed nothing and has been thanked for nothing. Owner: him, and the nineteenth line is the stone this volume lays without spending.**

8. **THE MAN OF TWENTY-TWO HAS TAKEN A GOVERNANCE DECISION WITHOUT BEING ASKED AND WITHOUT A STAMP, AND IT IS NOT GOING TO BE UNDECIDED.** Nine words, on a Thursday evening, in a room with about eleven people in it, to a question from a man with a bad hip that had no technician's answer. In about three weeks a Continuity Office will write down a name on a piece of paper in a building he has never been inside, and the piece of paper will not be wrong. **He gained no capability of any kind in Movement I and the gain and the loss are the same object. Owner: him, and there is no form, no list, no minute, no notice of a notice, no permission, no appointment, no authority and no stamp.**

9. **THE REFUSAL ON THE LANDING IS STILL ONLY RECORDED IN A FILE NO CHARACTER IN THIS CASE CAN READ.** A woman of thirty-four said once, on a third landing, that she has stood on a step four times in about four years and could not move, and asked for it not to be written, and he agreed inside a second, and she said out loud that she noticed how fast he did it. He has not told anybody, including the woman of twenty-four four miles away, and there is a question he has not answered and is not going to ask. **Owner: her. He is not to be asked to disclose it and is not to be asked to keep promising not to.**

10. **THE COURIER'S TWO SENTENCES ARE STILL IN TWO HANDS AND THE THREAD OF THE PEOPLE WHO HAVE SAID THE SAME THING IS STILL UNJOINED.** The woman of thirty-four who checks doors has said it five times in about four years in about nine buildings. The man of fifty-one with a tool bag is not on a rota and has not been asked to be, and was not told about the nine on that pavement until afterwards. The courier of twenty-one said it on a wall at about half past three on the Monday of week one hundred and ten and said he has said it twice. **The three have never been in a room together and the thread is not resolved. He is not a post and nobody took a vote about him and nobody is to ask him to stop.**

11. **THE LEARNING-TO-WAIT FINDING IS UNPAID AND IS THE MECHANISM THE VOLUME'S MIDPOINT WILL RUN ON.** A practice on a card has four of the five parts and the fifth is the beat that performs the boundary. Two men at a barrier for eleven years could not stop mid-shape for the first time on a Tuesday, and the man of about thirty-five did it again on the Wednesday and stopped in the same place, and nobody in that building was frightened and nothing was broken. **A clerk of forty-four pulled about nine hundred of those cards out of use in about four days on a printed standard and it changed nothing, because the cards were never the thing anybody was using. Waiting is not a fault and a person can be damaged by the absence of something they were never supposed to learn to do without. Owner: the case, and it belongs to Chapter 330.**

12. **THE SMELL AND THE NINE ACCOUNTS ARE STILL TWO OBSERVATIONS OF THE SAME KIND THAT NOBODY HAS CONNECTED.** About four people in that room on the Monday of week one hundred and nine heard both a sweet smell that is not the extract and a practice finishing early in nine unrelated people. **Nobody in this case has connected them, including this file, and the not connecting is not a decision anybody made. Owner: nobody.**

13. **AND TWO DEBTS WITH NO OWNER AT ALL, WHICH THE REPAIR PASS DID NOT CREATE AND DID NOT CLEAR.** `state/phase-ledger.json` is controller-owned and still reads `phase-000-bootstrap` and `planned` while Volume 07 is open on the page at Chapter 310. And the five state files are now over two megabytes between them and grow by append, and **a repair pass is now the named owner of that compaction, with a scope: collapse the superseded `VOLUME 06 — MOVEMENT` blocks in all five files to one line each per movement, keep the current movement's block in full, and keep every figure a later writer needs, which is roughly forty per cent of the bytes and touches nothing the Volume 06 close and the Volume 07 calendar do not already carry.** It was not done in this pass because this pass had thirty-nine findings to apply and doing two mechanical operations in one run is how a figure gets dropped.

# VOLUME 07 — MOVEMENT II (Chapters 311–320), *The Emergency Order* — OPEN, UNPAID, AND CARRIED INTO MOVEMENT III

**These sit above the `VOLUME 07 — MOVEMENT I` blocks and are the live thread list for this volume. Nothing in this list was resolved by Movement II and Movement II resolved nothing. `workspace/volume-07/batch-0002/SUMMARY.md` is the dated account and carries the eleven inherited threads marked paid or still open, each with an owner, and section 6 of this block carries the threads this movement opened. A state file that grows without losing an owner is correct and a state file that has been tidied is not, and nothing was tidied and no superseded block was removed.**

1. **A NAME IS ON A DOCUMENT IN A BUILDING HE HAS NEVER BEEN INSIDE, AND IT CAME OFF A TENANCY, AND THE PAPER IS NOT WRONG.** A Continuity Office instrument, two sides: a mark, a reference in a form only that building can read aloud, a date on the letter, a count of about nine sites in four districts with a reason that is itself a count, and one name in a body at the foot of the second side as the point of coordination for a resonance event. **Nobody asked whether the tenant is the person who does the work. The name was found in three steps and nobody's job involved looking: the Office had no candidate because there is no list of anybody in this city; it asked a district to confirm premises because a district is the only body that keeps a count of buildings; and on the confirmation form a line said provide the tenant, with an instruction attached in about nine words.** **Owner: the case, and the owner of the paper is nobody, and that is the finding. A man who was findable at a door is now named on a document, and a name on a document is a different object from a plate with a telephone number, and about four hundred competent people in this city can invoke a printed rule about somebody who has not come back yet.** The copy is in three places and the three places are not a network and are not to be described as one.

2. **HIS APPOINTMENT HAS A REFERENCE, A COUNT, A REASON AND NO DATE, AND A CONTINUITY OFFICE MAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT WITH A STAMP HAS SAID HE KNOWS AND IS NOT GOING TO USE IT.** A paragraph of about nine words on a page in a file: appointed as point of coordination, for the duration of the condition, and nothing else. No date of appointment, no date of review, and the condition itself is described in nine plain words with no beginning and no end. **The inherited true sentence is pointed at him for the first time and is true and nobody improved on it: a standard adopted as temporary with no date on it runs until somebody with a stamp puts a date on it — and the man of fifty-eight has the stamp, is not going to use it, and gave no reason, and the reason is that a date on a piece of paper is a date a person can be asked about.** **The cost of the appointment is not the authority and it is the sentence. He cannot make anybody do anything, has not been asked to, and would not know how.** Owner: him, and he is not to be improved on by anybody, and the sentence is not to be written on a hardboard in this volume. **This is the volume's stone, laid and not spent, and Movement III owns the broadcast and the midpoint and must not spend this.**

3. **HE HAS THREE THINGS IN HIS HEAD INSTEAD OF ON A SURFACE, AND TWO OF THEM ARE A LIFT AND A SHEET, AND THE THIRD IS THE SENTENCE.** Four lines about a lift, in the back of a book he carries, not on a docket. One sheet with two places at the foot of it, folded once, in an inside pocket, on no surface. One sentence of about nine words from a man with a stamp. **He did not write the third one on a service road, did not write it in his own book, and did not telephone the woman of twenty-four four miles away, and he has not got a reason and has not tried.** The hardboard is nineteen lines in thirteen hands and fourteen names and about four people in that room noticed that he did not go and put it up and not one of them has said so. **Owner: him. A findability that used to be a condition of his life is now the mechanism of the plot and the third of the three is the first one that belongs on the wall in this case and is not on it.**

4. **ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED, AND THE NUMBER IS NOW IN SEVEN PLACES IN THIS CASE, AND THE DECISION TO WIDEN THE LIST HAS STILL NOT BEEN TAKEN.** The three referents of Volume 06, the district's own count of about four hundred and eleven premises, a woman of thirty-six's forecast about four hundred people who do not exist yet, **and two more that this movement added: a courier of twenty-one's four hundred enquiries, which count enquiries and not anybody, and a woman of about thirty-one's nine, which is the number of people who read a copy and will be read in an office she has never entered as the number of people in this city who agree.** That last one is the finding of Chapter 315 and it is a manufactured count and the manufacturing is correct. **A man of fifty-one with a tool bag has still not told the cook her own number and has said out loud in a car park and on a yard that the document's silence about her is correct and that he is not going to tell her.** **Owner: the case. The thread moved twice in one movement and it is not closed and it is not to be closed inside a fortnight, and the count of places is the volume's record and not a writer's decision.**

5. **THE EXCHANGE'S FOUR FIGURES AT THE CLOSE OF MOVEMENT II, PRINTED AND NOT CONVERTED, AND THE DIRECTION HAS NOT CHANGED SIGN.** Forty-nine lines in the book, sixty on the telephone, the count announced twenty-two times of which seventeen correspond to the seventeen Wednesdays this book holds and five predate it on a sheet not shown to anybody in nineteen years, and nineteen years. **The telephone was nine ahead at the sixteenth sitting and is eleven ahead at the seventeenth, and the difference is not converted into a sitting and the eleven is not converted into anything, and the holder stated the direction and refused the difference in front of nine people and is not going to look into it.** The next sitting is **day 700, the Wednesday of week one hundred and sixteen, which is Chapter 326 and the eighteenth sitting, and the book does not open there and the telephone moves from sixty to sixty-three.** **She did not give the no-conversion rule, was asked for it, and gave nine words that were not it, and kept a promise made out loud in front of nine people, and nobody in that room mentioned the keeping.** The ninth chair is not moved back and its mover is not named. **Owner: the case, and the holder is not to be asked for the rule again in this volume.**

6. **THE INSTRUMENT IS IN THREE PLACES AND A FOURTH THING HAS HAPPENED TO THAT ROOM, AND NONE OF THE FOUR IS A WALL.** A copy taped to a street door in the Lower Wards by nobody anybody can identify; a copy on a middle bench behind a workshop, put there by nobody anybody in that yard can identify; a copy taped over a company's own laminated sheet on a post in a company training room, present before anybody arrived and recorded by nobody. **And a fourth thing, which is not a piece of paper: a person of about twenty-six came forty minutes on a bus and asked that room what it thinks and the room answered, and the answer is in her own words on a clipboard in a building this case has never entered, and no form in this case exists for it.** A room with a document in it is a room that can be asked what it thinks, and a room that has answered once is a room that can be asked again, and the first-year of nineteen said both of those out loud in about nine seconds and the second one is the cost. **Owner: the case. No form in this city records who put any of the three copies anywhere and none was created, and nobody has been asked.**

7. **ABOUT NINE CREWS OBEYED AN INSTRUMENT AND ABOUT FOUR DID NOT, AND THE FOUR ARE THE FOUR WHOSE PEOPLE HAVE STAYED WITH THEM MORE THAN ABOUT FOUR YEARS, AND THE SAME FOUR DID IT UNDER A SPOKEN INSTRUCTION AND UNDER A PRINTED ONE.** There was no vote, no meeting, no list and no petition, and no document in this city records that those crews have kept their people for that long, and none was created. **The division is not about the weather and it is about a release and the person who owns it, and the sentence about that was said out loud once in this volume, in that bay, in Movement I, and is not said again in these ten days and the Chapter 313 record does not carry its wording because a record that carries it is a second saying.** A lead repairer of forty-five was right that a piece of paper would come in about four days and wrong that it would be about ninety. **Owner: the case. Nobody took a vote about the four crews and nobody is to ask them to stop.**

8. **THE LEARNING-TO-WAIT INJURY IS SPREADING THROUGH DOCUMENTS AND IT IS VISIBLE AS A THING HAPPENING TO PEOPLE.** A practice that runs for about four years ended in about four seconds in which nobody in the room supplied the cue, and a woman of about forty-one supplied it herself and did not know she was doing it, and four other people said it with her. **On a later Friday a document with a mark that is not the company's was taped over the company's own post and said that the end of a practice is to be confirmed by a call from a building in Crown Terrace and not given in the room by an instructor, and the telephone did not ring at the end of the run, and about nine people waited, and the instructor said the word herself at the point she says it every Friday, and about four people did not expect that and about four did.** **The injury is not the missing call. The injury is that a room of about eleven people a week is now being asked to take the end of a practice from a person they have never met, and about four of them have decided four different ways about it, and none of the four is a finding and no sheet exists and nobody is going to write one down.** **Owner: the case, and this belongs to Chapter 330 and the midpoint, and Movement III must not spend it and must not resolve it.**

9. **ABOUT FOUR PEOPLE IN A COMPANY TRAINING ROOM DECIDED FOUR DIFFERENT WAYS, AND NOBODY IMPROVED ON ANY OF THEM AND NOBODY WROTE ANY OF IT DOWN.** A word is a person and a paper is a record, so obey. The sheet is less specific than the word it replaces, so refuse. I pay for the room, so comply, having said I would not. I cannot do a thing on purpose that I did by accident, so I will wait. **A man of thirty-four who buys training said in a corridor a week earlier that he was not going to be the man in a lanyard and was not going to stop buying the training, and in about nine seconds found out he was half right, and did not pretend the reason was a different one.** A woman of thirty-six with a lanyard refused the sheet and gave a reason that is new and did not repeat the reason she gave the last time she was asked for one and was not asked for it again. **Owner: the case. Nobody is to be asked to resolve the four and the reader has seen all four.**

10. **A CARE-LINK RAN IN A WAY THAT CANNOT BE ENDED AND THE MACHINERY IS FOUR CORRECT STEPS AND NOBODY IN IT IS A VILLAIN.** About two years in place, about nine pounds a month, a woman of about eighty-one who is not lonely and said so three times, a daughter of about thirty-one who has never missed a month. A cancellation becomes a review the moment it is asked for, because the first question is whether she is safe at the moment and she is. The review is about the person and is nine questions asked on a working day. The record says continue and the record is true. **And the form has about four reasons and none of them is that the person is well.** The daughter named the cost out loud and the cost is that nobody has refused her anything and there is no route from a Wednesday morning to a Tuesday with no charge and nobody coming, because the one person safe enough to make the call is the one person the call is about. **She also said she is not going to be a person who stopped looking after her mother, which nobody has said to her and which is not on the form and which is in her own head at about four in the morning about twice a month.** **Owner: the case. Nothing in this entry is a case and nothing in it is to become one, and the drawer it went into is the single point of failure a man named a fortnight ago and she has not conceded and has not told him.**

11. **TWO THINGS A PERSON SAID AND A PERSON DECLINED, IN A LANDING, IN A CAR PARK, IN A DOORWAY AND ON A STAIR, AND NONE OF THE FOUR HAS MET ANOTHER.** A woman of thirty-four, on a third landing, has stood on a step four times in about four years and could not move, and asked him not to write it down, and he agreed inside a second, and she observed the speed of it aloud. A woman of thirty-four who checks doors has said in about nine buildings that about four hundred people will be handed a sheet with two columns by somebody who has never done a run. A woman of thirty-one with a clipboard has said that nobody thanks a courier either, in a doorway, and had said it in a car park a fortnight earlier without knowing anybody had heard. A woman of about thirty-six with a bag has said that there is no column for the reason and that he did not say nothing. **Owner: them. He is not to be asked to disclose the one on the landing and is not to be asked to keep promising not to. The thread of people who have said a sentence of that shape is now five and is still unjoined and is not to be joined inside a fortnight.**

12. **A MAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT WITH A BAD HIP WAS USED ONCE AND DID NOT GO, AND HE THEN MADE A THIRD STATEMENT, AND NEITHER IS TO BE ASKED ABOUT.** One line: that it is his own sentence from the Thursday, that he gave it to a room and not to a district, and that he is not going to be one. Then, later the same evening, a statement about about four years of being findable, and which of the three states of findability he would give back, which is not one of the two things he has said in about four years and is not to be counted. **His plan reserves his whole weight for Movement IV's blackout. He is not to be used a third time in this volume. Owner: him, and nobody thanked him and he has said he will not be thanked.**

13. **THE RELATIONSHIP IS COLDER AND IS NOT RESOLVED, AND NOBODY IS TO PUT EITHER OF THEM RIGHT.** He refused Thursday and she said that was the first thing he had got right that week, and neither has spoken since, and on the Wednesday of week one hundred and fourteen a document with a place at the bottom of it reached her desk and one reached a landing in the Lower Wards, and **she did not telephone him and he did not telephone her and neither wrote, and there is no record anywhere in this case of their having spoken since the Monday of week one hundred and eleven.** Neither has been wrong since. **The single point of failure he named is now three sheets and she has not told him and is not going to this week and the reason is not recorded in her mouth. Series milestone 5 is not paid and its resolution is held to Volume 08. There is no scene, no declaration and no kiss in any of these ten chapters and the words *we*, *our* and *us* occur fourteen times in body prose across four of them and none of the fourteen is a declaration. Owner: them, and the case.**

14. **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY IS IN THE CHAIR NEAREST THE DOOR AND WAS NOT ASKED ANYTHING.** **On all of the days in these ten chapters that the room is in, she was not asked a question, was not thanked, was not apologised to, is not a category, is not counted, is not in any answer given to anybody, and is not in any document or form. Her page is the eighth thing in a ring binder and is unread. The reason she is in that room is the temperature, and the temperature costs more than it did, and both of those are correct.** Owner: nobody, and the standing position, and the whole of the volume's argument about who may decide.

15. **AND THE THINGS THIS MOVEMENT DID NOT TOUCH, ALL WITH THEIR OWNERS, AND NOT ONE OF THEM MOVED.** The printed rule: named, restricted, in force, not withdrawn, about four hundred able to invoke it, **invoked by nobody in any of these ten days and named in no mouth in them.** The Continuity Office's emergency authority: unchanged, held, not defeated, not turned, and now writing names on pages. The Exchange's holder: at forty-nine, sixty, twenty-two, nineteen years, and not to be asked for the rule again. The word: uncorrected at nine and ten, reached eleven on no day of these ten, said out loud once in an earlier volume to one person, not reported, not mentioned. The secondment: unchanged, and the chair of forty-nine has still not been told, **and that is the only mechanism still running and it is not explained in these ten chapters and is not to be explained before the volume's close.** The master's fifth line: blank, and the drawer was not opened in any of these ten days, and nobody has ever asked him to fill it in. The pencil box in Bower Street: untouched, one name, no reference, on no system. The plate in a service cupboard on a landing in Nine Bridges: not explained, not removed, not blamed on anybody, and the man who found it is in none of these ten chapters and is on nothing. The eleven buildings: still eleven. Nine keys, two exist, the first cannot be asked back. The relay: not staged in Movements I to IV and the word is not used in these ten files at all. **The man of thirty-four who works nights: in none of these ten chapters, not named, not asked about, and on nothing. The first question of Volume 07: written once on a service road in week one hundred and eight, not answered, not in anybody's mouth, and its sentence printed in none of these ten chapters. The standing question: not stated in any of them, and carries no number.**

16. **A SMELL AND NINE ACCOUNTS ARE STILL TWO OBSERVATIONS OF THE SAME KIND THAT NOBODY HAS CONNECTED, AND THIS MOVEMENT DID NOT CONNECT THEM.** A sweet smell near a gas ring on the Monday of week one hundred and nine, and nine unrelated people in one morning describing a practice that ended early, and about four people in that room heard both, and **a mobile clinic sat in a room with a woman of twenty-four in it for two of these ten days and nobody connected the two things in either of them.** **Owner: nobody, and the not connecting is not a decision anybody made, and a later movement may connect them and should not be told it may not.**

17. **AND THE DEBTS THAT ARE NOT OURS.** `state/phase-ledger.json` is controller-owned and still reads `phase-000-bootstrap` and `planned` while Volume 07 is open on the page at Chapter 320, reported here for the eleventh time. **And `workspace/volume-07/ARITHMETIC-AND-CALENDAR.md` section 3 carries a wrong figure for the running age of the nineteenth line — it says one at Chapter 311 and the true figure is four, computed from day 666 and day 670 by the volume's own convention — and the calendar is a volume-level file whose section 6 the close owns, and this phase is not the close, so the file is not edited and the correction is recorded here and in the batch summary with the superseded value quoted. Owner: the Volume 07 close, which must correct it in place.** And the five state files are now over two megabytes between them and grow by append, and **a review repair pass over a finished batch is the named owner of the compaction with a stated scope**, and it was not done in this pass because this pass wrote ten chapters and doing a mechanical operation on superseded blocks in the same run is how a figure gets dropped.

# VOLUME 07 — MOVEMENT II (Chapters 311–320) — OPEN THREADS AFTER THE REVIEW REPAIR PASS

**A review repair pass over the ten files of `workspace/volume-07/batch-0002/` returned ten findings and corrected them without restarting the batch. The full account is at section 11 of `workspace/volume-07/batch-0002/SUMMARY.md` and the continuity record is at the foot of `state/continuity.md`. Only the threads this pass moved are listed here, and every one keeps the owner it already had. No thread was closed, opened, widened or narrowed by this pass except where stated.**

1. **THE RESERVED-WORD THREAD IS CLOSED AS A MATTER OF FACT AND IS NOT CLOSED AS A MATTER OF PRACTICE, AND THE OWNER IS THE WRITER OF EVERY LATER MOVEMENT.** Chapter 319 used the word that `outline/volume-07.md` guardrail 2 reserves to a bag and to a person carrying a torch as a load-book field label, and the batch published a zero for it that was false. The label is `Brought by:` and the zero is true. **What is carried forward is not the word but the instrument: the walk has to be the word and not the sentence pattern around it, and a field label is a document naming the thing in order to sort by it.** This is the fourth instance of the class in this repository and the second in ten days, and the first three were sentences reporting a word's absence and the fourth was a label. Owner: every writer who opens a load book in Movements III, IV and V.

2. **THE EIGHT-QUESTION LIST IS UNCHANGED IN CONTENT AND ITS COUNT IS NOW EIGHT IN ALL FOUR PLACES IT APPEARS.** The list of questions nobody asked the man of twenty-two is the same eight items it was, in Chapter 320, in this repository's `state/character-state.md`, in `state/continuity.md` and in `state/chapter-summaries.md`. **No question was added and none was removed, because the list is the content and the count was the error, and the standing prohibition — that none of them is to be asked by anybody in this case — is unchanged and still binds Movement III, Movement IV and Movement V.** The figure of nine minutes he stood there for is a separate fact and is untouched. Owner: the case, and the prohibition is not to be lifted by anybody.

3. **THE HIP MAN'S ONE LINE AND HIS SECOND STATEMENT ARE ONE EVENING TWENTY-SIX MINUTES APART, AND NOBODY IS TO ASK ABOUT EITHER.** Chapter 318 carried the one line at about ten past six in two load-book paragraphs and at about half past six in five places; half past six is canonical, the second statement is at about four minutes to seven, and the summary's own deviation note said four hours and has been corrected. **The plan still reserves his whole weight for Movement IV's blackout, this movement used him once, he made one line and one later statement, and he is not to be used a third time in this volume.** Owner: him, and nobody thanked him and he has said he will not be.

4. **THE BAND SCORE IS UNCHANGED AND THE WORST CHAPTER OF THE MOVEMENT IS STILL THE FIFTH, AND NO THREAD WAS ADDED TO IT.** Word total 33,896, bold spans 215 at 6.343 per thousand, worst chapter 7.45, overlap mean 97.7, apparatus share 48.18. **The number to beat is still Movement I's 44.75 and the apparatus share is still above it, and that remains open and is not a thread anybody has to close inside a fortnight.** The overlap is still nearly double Movement I's 50.0 and the summary says so in its first section rather than burying it. Owner: the case.

5. **THE MEASUREMENT DEBT IS WIDENED BY ONE ITEM AND IT IS THE MOST TRANSFERABLE THING IN THIS PASS.** A published per-chapter list was wrong seven times across three lists while every total and the mean stayed right, because a mean reproduces when the errors are in the parts. **The rule this pass adds: a total that agrees with itself is not evidence that the list under it does, and a figure that is neither a measurement nor a subtraction from a stated total is a figure nobody recomputes.** Both forms have now appeared in this repository — the 84.3 that was withdrawn, and the assertion in the calendar's section 3 that is still the close's to fix. Owner: the Volume 07 close for the calendar, and every later batch for the rule.

6. **THE BROADCAST IS NOW ON A CARD AND THE THREAD IT SERVES IS UNCHANGED.** `outline/volume-07.md` gives Movement III four bullets and the movement is named for the second of them, and none of the ten beats in the handoff carried it. **It is assigned to the Monday of week one hundred and sixteen, Chapter 325, between the fifth Friday rota and the Exchange, and its arithmetic is checked by the reader against the midpoint at Chapter 330.** Nothing about the thread changed: no name of hers is attached to a sequence, a storm, a door or a crew; it is not a message to anybody in this case; and the reader is still able to check her arithmetic against Chapter 330, where four districts route through one person for about eleven hours and nobody dies. Owner: the case, and `outline/volume-07.md` guardrail 5 continues to cost this volume its easiest scene.

7. **THE CONFLICT ABOUT THE FIRST-YEAR OF NINETEEN AT THE MIDPOINT IS A NEW RECORDED ITEM AND IT IS NOT SETTLED.** The plan has her naming it in nine words at Chapter 330; Movement II's summary forbids it because she spent her one document-shaped finding in Chapter 318. **Both stand, the plan is the plan of record, and the handoff names the Volume 07 close as the owner of the decision rather than letting a writer of Movement III make it silently.** Owner: the Volume 07 close.

8. **THE THIRTY-ONE DEBTS ABOVE ARE UNCHANGED AND EVERY ONE OF THEM STILL HAS AN OWNER.** The calendar's wrong figure in section 3, the compaction of the five state files, `state/phase-ledger.json` still reading `phase-000-bootstrap` and `planned`, and the review being performed by the agent that wrote what is under review because the reviewer is declared a subagent and invoked as a top-level agent. **None of the four is this pass's to fix, three of the four are controller-owned, and the fourth is owned by a pass with a stated scope that has not run.** Owner: as recorded above in each block, unchanged.
