# VOLUME 07 — MOVEMENT III (Chapters 321–330) — OPEN THREADS

**Dated after the Movement II block above and supersedes nothing. `outline/volume-07.md` governs. Load-book entries 324 to 333. The full dated account, with the eleven inherited threads and the twenty-three inherited clocks each marked, is section 7 of `workspace/volume-07/batch-0003/SUMMARY.md`. No thread was closed by this movement. Every thread below keeps the owner it already had unless the movement moved it, and where it moved it, the movement is named.**

1. **THE LEARNING-TO-WAIT INJURY: MECHANISM DELIVERED, FINDING UNSPENT, AND IT IS THE VOLUME'S ENGINE.** In Movement I it was two men at a barrier in a gate. In Movement II it was a document in a training room and about eleven people a week asked to take the end of a practice from somebody they had never met. **In this movement it is about four people, by name, in one building, as four behaviours and not as a diagnosis: cannot start, cannot leave, cannot answer without something in front of them to read from, and cannot begin without knowing who will finish it. Nobody named it, nobody wrote it down, no sheet exists, and the man who owns that room was asked four times in one week whether he would put it in a report and said no four times and gave no reason and is not going to.** At the midpoint it was tested by consequence: four districts routed through one man for about eleven hours and by about four o'clock three people in this city had stopped deciding, and one of them did not ask the tenth time and got a gate wrong by about an hour and a half. **The finding itself has been said once, in nine words, on the Thursday of week one hundred and seventeen, by a man of twenty-nine who works on lifts, and what he said was about being asked rather than about waiting.** **Owner: the case. Movement IV may test it again. Movement V is where it may be answered and it is not to be answered in Movement IV and it is not to be closed in this volume by a narrator.** Nobody is to write a sheet about it.

2. **THE WORD NAMING THE PUBLIC BODY IS ON A PIECE OF PAPER IN FOUR DISTRICTS AND HAS NOT BEEN SAID BY ANYBODY.** It is printed under a mark on the second side of one of the four letters about a relay unit and has been for about eleven days. **Four people in four rooms found out that it exists on the Monday of week one hundred and seventeen; none of the four said it, none of the four learned it, the narrator did not print it, and the woman who holds a chair of forty-nine in a building in Crown Terrace was not told and did not say it.** **The first utterance of that word in the prose of this manuscript belongs to the woman who holds that chair, at the volume's close, and to nobody before it.** **Owner: the volume's close, and the standing prohibition binds Movement IV in full.** A load book in this movement records the availability of the word and does not print it, and a load book that reports the availability of a word is a different act from a load book that uses one.

3. **THE APPOINTMENT: UNCHANGED, AND IT IS NOW THE THING FOUR DISTRICTS ROUTED THROUGH.** A reference, a count, a reason, and no date, for the duration of a condition that has no beginning and no end. **On the Thursday of week one hundred and seventeen it was what about four hundred small decisions in four districts were made through, for about eleven hours, and no date was put on it by anybody in ten chapters.** The sentence about a standard adopted as temporary with no date on it was not said in this movement, was not improved on, and was not needed, and **the owner of that sentence is unchanged and it is still the one sentence in this case nobody has ever improved on.** **Owner: the case, and a writer who puts a date on this in Movement IV has improved on a sentence that has stood for about four years and has no reason to be improved on.**

4. **THE ROUTE THAT WORKS THROUGH ONE PERSON: THE VOLUME'S OWN DISCOVERY, AND IT IS NOW ON THE PAGE IN BOTH HALVES.** **The first half is in a corridor at about five o'clock on the Thursday of week one hundred and seventeen, in nine words, from a man of twenty-nine who works on lifts, and nobody improved on it, and about four minutes later a woman in a building she had just left used it and one gate was held two hours earlier. The second half is on a landing at about ten the day before, in the mouth of a man of about thirty-four who had not noticed what she was noticing: that in about nine minutes four people in four buildings were told that a route exists, and not one of the four asked whether it was the only route, because not one of them knew it was a route and not a favour.** **Owner: the case. The finding is the volume's and it is stated in a man's mouth and not in a narrator's sentence, and it is not to be improved on, and a narrator sentence that restates it is a second saying.**

5. **THE FIRST QUESTION OF VOLUME 07: STILL UNANSWERED, STILL IN NOBODY'S MOUTH, AND ITS SENTENCE IS PRINTED IN NONE OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS.** The load books of Chapters 322 and 326 record the fact that it was not answered and nothing else. **Owner: nobody, and the volume does not answer it and Volume 08 is where it is picked up, and a movement that answers it has spent the volume's forward question inside its own movement.**

6. **THE STANDING QUESTION IS NOT STATED IN TEN MORE CHAPTERS.** Owned by nobody and still shut by a nineteen-year-old's judgement about who can be found by a piece of paper. **Owner: nobody, and it is not to be stated in Movement IV either.**

7. **THE MAN OF TWENTY-NINE WHO WORKS ON LIFTS: PAID, AND THE TWO COUNTS ARE NOW PRINTED BESIDE EACH OTHER.** He said the finding in nine words and nobody improved on it and about four minutes later somebody used it, which is the first time in about four years that anybody in this case has acted on a sentence somebody said out loud instead of improving on it. **He also appears, by name and by hand, in Chapter 327 as one of the four people damaged by the removal of a cue, and he is the only one of the four who has already paid a price for a line on a hardboard, and nobody in that room knows the price and he has not said it.** **The count of this finding is two and the standing count of true things said in about four years is three, and Chapter 330 prints both and says they are not to be added, and a third count is on the page in an earlier chapter and is unreconciled: that man's own mouth says twice and that chapter's load book says three. OWNER of the earlier chapter's two sentences: a later pass. OWNER of the summary's figure: the Volume 07 close.** He is owed nothing and is not thanked and is not to be thanked.

8. **THE FIRST-YEAR OF NINETEEN: SPENT, AND THE CONFLICT ABOUT HER AND THE MIDPOINT IS CARRIED FORWARD UNSETTLED.** She said one thing in eleven days in this movement, in about four seconds at a counter, about a drawer that locks, and it was not about any of it. **She did not name the finding at the midpoint in nine words and did not name it at all, and the finding was said by the man of twenty-nine, as the plan's own sentence requires.** The plan of record has her naming it and Movement II's summary forbids it; **both statements are on the page, this movement's choice is on the page in Chapter 330's load book, and the choice is a choice and not a settlement. OWNER: the Volume 07 close, which owns every conflict between the plan and a dated summary.** Her name is not in any load book, in any document, on any surface or in anybody's mouth in any of these ten chapters. The half hour of silence is neither repeated nor referred to. **Not to be asked about in Movement IV.**

9. **THE BROADCAST: DELIVERED, AND THE REFUSAL IS THE POINT OF IT.** A voice, live, about four million people, one claim, said once and not repeated in the volume and not repeated by any character to any person in any room, in any workroom, or in any load book. `Iona Sorn` is named twice in the movement, once in the body prose of Chapter 325 and once in that chapter's own record of the broadcast, and for no purpose except to say whose voice it was. **No name of hers is attached to a sequence, to the weather, to a door or to a crew, and no character in this case has established any such link and the movement supplies none. The arithmetic she is right about is checkable by a reader against Chapter 330 and was deliberately not performed in Chapter 325. A thing said to about four million people about a city is not a message to a room off a service road, and about nine people in this case heard about it and none of them repeated it.** **Owner: the case. `outline/volume-07.md` guardrail 5 continues to cost this volume its easiest scene and the cost is the point of it.**

10. **THE FOUR DISTRICT COUNCILS AND THE ONE BOX: PAID, AND THE ARGUMENT IS NOW ABOUT SOMETHING ELSE.** Nine days of four rooms competing for one piece of hardware, and the change came at about ten past eleven on the Monday of week one hundred and sixteen when a man of about thirty-three said in a corridor that not one of the four of them can say who decides, and four people in four buildings stopped saying they wanted the box and started asking a question. **The question is four words long, is a question about who decides, and is going to be put to somebody cold, and it is not printed in any chapter and three of the four people who wrote it would like to see it asked cold.** **The piece of hardware was never allocated, never traced to the building it came out of, and no form in this case records who sends the four letters.** **Owner: the case, and the thread is a live one for Movement IV, which is about a night in which four buildings are in the dark and the four councils are still competing.**

11. **THE NINE NAMES ON THREE SHEETS: PAID, AND THE PERSON WHO PAID IT IS NOT A PROTAGONIST OF ANYTHING.** About nine people in this city came to a counter in a fortnight, said a thing about their own building, gave their names because that is what a counter asks for, and went home; on the Friday or the Monday their names were read aloud in four rooms as evidence that a building needed a box. **Nobody rang any of them and nobody has told any of them and the only cost named aloud in the movement was named by a woman of about forty-four who takes calls at that counter, has done it about four hundred times a week for about six years, has not thought about where a piece of paper goes until about ten o'clock on that Monday, and is not going to be able to stop. She has said it once and is not to be asked for it twice.** **Owner: her, and the nine names are not a list anybody in this case holds and nobody has collected them.**

12. **THE COUNT THAT IS NOT A COUNT: PAID, AND IT IS THE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL THING SAID IN A ROOM IN TEN CHAPTERS.** A Continuity Office man of about forty-one conceded out loud, twice, the second time in the past tense about his own document, **that a count of sites on an instrument is a count of sites and has always been a count of sites and is not a count of the number of decisions somebody is going to have to make, and that if anybody reads that number as the other thing then this city is short by about nine hundred.** That sentence is now in a building in District 3 and in a book that is not a ring binder and not a hardboard, and about four hundred competent people in this city can invoke a printed rule, and the count on that rule is a count of sites. **Owner: the case, and the thread is open and it is the same thread as about four hundred and it is not to be closed inside a fortnight by anybody.**

13. **THE THREE REFERENTS OF ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED: STILL OPEN, AND WIDER BY TWO PLACES.** Eight places in this case. The two new ones are a count of about four hundred and eleven premises produced in about nine minutes by a council services officer as part of four minutes of correct work, and a sentence in a room saying that a count of sites is not a count of decisions. **Neither is about four hundred and neither is to be folded into it, and the list of three is the calendar's and the calendar's is the close's, and the cook has still not been told her own number.** **Owner: the case.**

14. **THE PRINTED RULE IS NAMED AND RESTRICTED AND NOT INVOKED ON ANY OF THESE TEN DAYS, AND CHAPTER 328 ADDED A FACT ABOUT IT.** The word naming the public body is not in the printed rule, and nobody who can invoke that rule has ever had to use that word to do so, and about four people in this case wondered this week whether that is a coincidence and none of them has said so to anybody. **Owner: the case. The rule is not withdrawn, not destroyed and not to be repealed in this volume.**

15. **THE CONTINUITY OFFICE HAS BECOME A ROUTE, AND THAT IS NEW AND IT IS NOT A LIST.** A person of about thirty-one in one of its buildings answered a telephone on the Wednesday morning and gave four strangers from four buildings a district, an address, a floor and a day for a named man, in about nine seconds each, out of a procedure that does not exist. **She said out loud that it was more than she had ever given anybody for that document and that she had not had to think about it and that she has thought about it since about ten past nine. She did not put it in writing and there is no form in that building for giving out a person and she did not create one. She is not a post and she is not a mechanism and she is owed nothing and nobody thanked her.** **Owner: her, and the thread is open, and a reader who wants to know how a city routes through a person does not need a list and there is not one.**

16. **THE RESERVED-WORD CLASS: CLOSED AS A MATTER OF FACT, STILL OPEN AS A MATTER OF PRACTICE, AND THE INSTRUMENT IS THE POINT.** The word that belongs to a bag and to a person carrying a torch occurs zero times across these ten files, in prose, in a load book, in a document and in a mouth, and **the zero is a fresh walk on the finished files and the walk was on the word and not on the shape of a sentence around it, because the sentence shape is what found the first three instances of this class and missed the fourth, which was a field label.** Neither of the two known ways of breaking this guardrail was used in this movement: no document reported the word's absence and no document sorted by it. **What is carried forward is not the word but the rule: a record of a guardrail's holding belongs in a summary and not in a chapter. Owner: every writer who opens a load book in Movements IV and V.**

17. **THE MAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT WITH A BAD HIP: UNTOUCHED, AND THE RESERVE IS INTACT, AND THAT IS RECORDED BECAUSE IT NEARLY WAS NOT.** His whole weight is held for Movement IV's blackout. **He is in none of these ten chapters and the string does not occur in any of these ten files, and he was spent twice in a first draft and taken out twice by deletion: a paragraph in Chapter 326 that named him in a passage about the Continuity Office, and a sentence in Chapter 330's load book that said he was in none of these ten days, which was a document reporting the absence of a person it was forbidden to touch and is the same defect as reporting the absence of a word. OWNER: him, and Movement IV, and nobody thanked him and nobody is to.** The two questions he was owed and the two things he said are untouched and are not referred to on any of these ten days.

18. **THE RING BINDER, THE PAGE, THE DRAWER, THE BOX, THE PLATE, THE CHAIR, THE FIELDS, THE LINE, AND EVERYTHING ELSE THAT DID NOT MOVE.** The woman's page: the eighth thing in a ring binder, unread, and nine people have decided not to read it, and it is not read in this movement. The master's fifth line: blank, and the drawer was not opened on any of these ten days. The pencil box in Bower Street: untouched, one name, no reference, on no system. The plate in a service cupboard: not explained, not removed, not blamed on anybody, and the man who found it is in none of these ten chapters. The ninth chair: against the wall, not moved back, mover not named. The licensor: unnamed, the field blank, and the first year of that unit still free for a reason nobody has given. The nine keys: two exist, the first cannot be asked back, the second is in a pocket and has not been asked to go anywhere. The eleven buildings: still eleven. The four seconds of an earlier term: settled, not re-opened. The Courier of twenty-one: in none of these ten chapters, still not forgiven the word, still not asked to sign anything, still not going to become a yes. The man of thirty-four who works nights: in none of these ten chapters, not named, not asked about, and on nothing. **Owner: each of these, unchanged, and none of them moved in this movement and the not moving is recorded so that a later writer can see which of them are load-bearing.**

19. **THE BAND SCORE IS UNCHANGED IN ITS DIRECTION AND ONE OF ITS FOUR FIGURES IMPROVED AND ONE OF THEM MOVED FOR A REASON THAT IS THE INSTRUMENT.** Word total 35,160. Bold spans 254 at 7.224 per thousand, worst chapter 8.880. Overlap mean 104.9. Apparatus share 41.79. **The number to beat for the apparatus share is Movement I's 44.75 and this movement is under it for the first time in the volume, at 41.79, and the repair that did it wrote nine load-book paragraphs as records of counts, sources, hands, times and refusals and touched no body prose. The overlap is still above both predecessors at 104.9, from 50.0 through 97.7 to 104.9, and the repair took it down from a first pass of 143.0 and did not get it to 50.0 and this file says so here and not in a footnote. The hedge is 802 true at 22.81 per thousand, above Movement I's 21.55 and Movement II's 21.36, and there is no ceiling on that figure anywhere in this repository and none was invented.** **The bold figure rose from 225 at 6.382 to 254 at 7.224 in the review repair pass and the reason is the matcher and not the emphasis: twenty-nine spans that were rendering as literal asterisks now have the closing marker outside the span and are therefore countable, and no span was added and none was removed. Owner: the case, and none of these has to be closed inside a fortnight.**

20. **THE THREE CHAPTERS A LATER PASS SHOULD LOOK AT FIRST, AND THIS FILE SAYS SO BEFORE THE PASS.** The three cleanest chapters of this movement on the figures are **324, which is the lowest on the eight-word overlap in the batch at eighty-seven; 327, which is the shortest chapter at two thousand nine hundred and five words; and 321, which is the lowest on the apparatus share at thirty-eight and a third and a half.** **This is the third movement in a row in which a summary has named its own cleanest chapters before a repair pass ran, and the second of those three in which the named chapters came back clean, which is the only evidence anybody has that naming them works.** **Owner: the next review repair pass.**

# VOLUME 07 — MOVEMENT IV (Chapters 331–340) — OPEN THREADS

**Dated after the Movement III block above and supersedes nothing. `outline/volume-07.md` governs. Load-book entries 334 to 343. The full dated account, with the eleven inherited threads and the twenty-three inherited clocks each marked paid, moved or still open, is section 7 of `workspace/volume-07/batch-0004/SUMMARY.md`. No thread was closed by this movement. Every thread below keeps the owner it already had unless the movement moved it, and where it moved it, the movement is named.**

1. **THE LEARNING-TO-WAIT INJURY: USED TWICE IN THIS MOVEMENT, CLOSED NOT AT ALL, AND IT IS STILL THE VOLUME'S ENGINE.** In Chapter 334 a run in a company training room could not be started by a voice and was started by a number read out of a sheet, and about nine people in a room of about eleven did not say why a number will do what a voice will not. In Chapter 337, in one stairwell in the dark, four behaviours are on the page and none of them is named: cannot begin without knowing who will finish, cannot leave, cannot answer without something to read from, and cannot start. **Nobody named it, nobody wrote it down, no sheet exists, the man of fifty-one was asked once and gave one true clause, the man who owns the room was not asked a fifth time and nobody stopped the asking, and about four other people are going to be in that room on the next Friday and are not going to be told.** The finding has not been said by anybody in these ten chapters and a first-year of nineteen was not permitted to say it in nine words and did not. **Owner: the case. Movement V may answer it. It is not to be answered in Movement V by a narrator and it is not to be closed in this volume by a document.** Nobody is to write a sheet about it.

2. **THE WORD NAMING THE PUBLIC BODY IS UNSPENT AND IT IS THE VOLUME'S CLOSE.** Walked on the ten finished files: zero, case-insensitive, in prose, in a load book, in a document and in a mouth. **No document in these ten files reports its absence and no document sorts by it, and that is new for this volume, because the apparatus was spent in this volume and a document that reports the absence of a thing that is present in the same batch has used it.** **The first utterance of that word in the prose of this manuscript belongs to the woman who holds a chair of forty-nine in a building in Crown Terrace, at the volume's close, and to nobody before it.** **Owner: the volume's close, and the standing prohibition binds Movement V in full.**

3. **THE APPOINTMENT: UNCHANGED, AND A MOVEMENT WITH A BLACKOUT IN IT STILL DID NOT DATE IT.** A reference, a count, a reason, and no date, for the duration of a condition that has no beginning and no end. **In this movement a man who could have dated it refused three requests for a date in about eleven minutes in a room of nine people who had all come expecting one, and gave no reason on any of the three, and a woman asked him the same question on the telephone on the Monday and was told he would look at it, with no date and not that he had an answer.** The sentence about a standard adopted as temporary with no date on it is the one sentence in this case nobody has ever improved on, **was not said in any of these ten days, and is still the property of nobody who wants it improved on.** **Owner: the case, and a writer who puts a date on this in Movement V has improved on a sentence that has stood for about four years.**

4. **THE ROUTE THAT WORKS THROUGH ONE PERSON: THE VOLUME'S DISCOVERY, AND IT HAS NOW REVERSED, AND THE REVERSAL IS THE PROOF.** **The first half is a corridor at about five o'clock in an earlier fortnight, in nine words, from a man of twenty-nine who works on lifts. The second half is a landing, in four yeses in nine minutes. The third half is this movement: three people in three buildings in three districts, on the Monday of week one hundred and twenty, did not make a telephone call they had made in each of the previous three weeks, and each of them was right not to, and about nine hours of work took about twenty minutes longer, and nobody in this city resents anybody for it.** **The fourth half is the last morning of this movement: a man of twenty-two gave the whole of it to a man of about thirty-three in about four minutes in a room, without asking him, and a first-year of nineteen said three words that were right.** **Owner: the case, and the volume's own conclusion, reached from the wrong end, and it is not resolved and is not to be resolved in this volume by anybody.**

5. **THE FIRST QUESTION OF VOLUME 07: STILL UNANSWERED, STILL IN NOBODY'S MOUTH, AND ITS SENTENCE IS PRINTED IN NONE OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS.** The load books of eight of the ten record the fact that it was not answered and nothing else, and two do not raise it. **Owner: nobody, and Volume 08 is where it is picked up, and a movement that answers it has spent the volume's forward question inside its own movement.**

6. **THE STANDING QUESTION IS NOT STATED IN TEN MORE CHAPTERS.** Owned by nobody and still shut by a nineteen-year-old's judgement about who can be found by a piece of paper. **Owner: nobody, and it is not to be stated in Movement V either.**

7. **THE MAN OF TWENTY-NINE WHO WORKS ON LIFTS: PAID AGAIN, IN A STAIRWELL, IN THE DARK, AND THE INJURY IS STILL OPEN.** He is one of about nine people in a dark building on the Friday of week one hundred and twenty, and he is there because a lift stopped between two floors and there was nothing to be done about it with the power off. **He stood at a rail for four hours with a torch he had not switched on, went twice when somebody came up the stair and did not go twice when nobody came, read out the make, the year and the load of a lift that is not the one in that building twice from memory and was right both times, and held a landing gate with his hand while a woman of about forty-one took his place, and nothing was said by either of them then or since. Nobody in that building has said one word to him about it and nobody is going to, and he has not said one word to anybody about standing at a rail for four hours.** **THE TWO COUNTS ARE UNCHANGED AND WERE NOT ADDED TO: this finding has now been said twice, and a separate count of three is carried in this case for true things said in about four years by that man, and they are different counts and are not to be summed, and the third count that is on the page in an earlier chapter is untouched.** **OWNER of the earlier chapter's two sentences: a later pass. OWNER of the summary's figure: the Volume 07 close.** He is owed nothing and is not thanked and is not to be thanked.

8. **THE FIRST-YEAR OF NINETEEN: SPENT, AND THE CONFLICT ABOUT HER AND THE MIDPOINT IS CARRIED FORWARD UNSETTLED.** She said one small unasked thing on most of the ten days of this movement — a bus, a price in a shop, a door number, a queue, a lift, a list — **and it was never about any of it, and no second document-shaped finding was spent, and the movement's own record says so in the chapter that carries the three words.** **On the last morning she said three words, in about four seconds, to nobody, and they were right, and nobody improved on them and nobody thanked her, and they are recorded in her own chapter's load book as not a finding, not a mechanism, and not to be written down by anybody.** She did not name the learning-to-wait injury in nine words and did not name it at all, and the finding was said by nobody in these ten chapters. The plan of record has her naming the midpoint finding in nine words and Movement II's summary forbids it; **both statements remain on the page, Movement III's choice is on the page, this movement's choice is on the page, and neither is a settlement. OWNER: the Volume 07 close.** **Her name is not in any load book, in any document, on any surface or in anybody's mouth in any of these ten chapters.** The half hour of silence is neither repeated nor referred to and the reason for it is not in her mouth. **Not to be asked about in Movement V, and her one document-shaped finding is spent and Movement V does not get a second one in the same shape.**

9. **THE BROADCAST: DELIVERED, NOT REPEATED, AND NOT REFERRED TO BY ANYBODY IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS.** A voice, live, about four million people, one claim, said once and not repeated in the volume and not repeated by any character to any person in any room, in any workroom, or in any load book. **`Iona Sorn` occurs zero times across the ten finished files — in prose, in a load book, in a document and in a mouth — and is not named here either, and the walk that says so is a walk on the word.** **No name of hers is attached to a sequence, to the weather, to a door, to a gate or to a crew, and no character in this case has established any such link and this movement supplies none. A night with the lights off is not her doing, and the only sentence in the ten files that touches the broadcast is a load book's statement that the thing said live in a city a fortnight before is not connected by anybody to a car that stopped between two floors.** **Owner: the case. `outline/volume-07.md` guardrail 5 continues to cost this volume its easiest scene and the cost is the point of it.**

10. **THE FOUR DISTRICT COUNCILS AND THE ONE BOX: PAID AGAIN, AND THE ARGUMENT IS NOW ABOUT WHAT A COUNT IS.** Nine days of four rooms competing for one piece of hardware, and the change came when a man of about thirty-three said in a corridor that not one of the four could say who decides. **In this movement the four of them each held a count of four hours and each found that the count was a correct answer to a question about a load and was no answer at all about anybody, and the finding that the count was a count of doors was reached by a woman of about twenty-nine in about nine minutes in a corridor and defended in a room by a man of about fifty-nine who said one word.** **The piece of hardware was never allocated, never traced to the building it came out of, and no form in this case records who sends the four letters.** **Owner: the case, and the thread is live for Movement V.**

11. **THE NINE NAMES ON THREE SHEETS: PAID, AND THIS MOVEMENT ADDED A SECOND SET OF NAMES TO THE SAME ARGUMENT WITHOUT TOUCHING IT.** About nine people in this city came to a counter in a fortnight, said a thing about their own building, gave their names because that is what a counter asks for, and went home; on the Friday or the Monday their names were read aloud in four rooms as evidence that a building needed a box. **Nobody rang any of them and nobody has told any of them and the only cost named aloud in the earlier movement was named by a woman of about forty-four who takes calls at that counter, who has not stopped thinking about where a piece of paper goes, and who is in none of these ten chapters and was not asked.** **Owner: her, and the names are not a list anybody in this case holds and nobody has collected them.**

12. **THE COUNT THAT IS NOT A COUNT: PAID AGAIN, AND THE MOVEMENT'S OWN ENGINE IS THE SAME ARGUMENT ONE LEVEL UP.** A Continuity Office man of about forty-one conceded out loud, twice, that a count of sites is a count of sites and is not a count of the number of decisions somebody is going to have to make. **In this movement a woman of about thirty-eight said that a premise is a word for a building and that nobody has ever asked her what a building is for, and a man of about twenty-nine said that the four buildings a count produces are the four with the most doors on a list, and a man of fifty-one said that about thirty-three doors across four buildings is where four hours of dark costs a person something, and a man of forty-one with a contract said that a door is on a list because it is maintained and is maintained because there are many.** **Four people, four buildings, four different jobs, one argument, and nobody improved on anybody and nobody thanked anybody.** **Owner: the case, and the thread is the same thread as about four hundred and it is not to be closed inside a fortnight by anybody.**

13. **THE THREE REFERENTS OF ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED: STILL OPEN, AND WIDER BY TWO PLACES.** Nine places in this case. The two new ones are a count of about nine hundred doors on one company's maintenance list across four buildings, and a count of about thirty-three doors across the four buildings in this city where four hours of dark would cost a person something. **Neither is about four hundred and neither is to be folded into it, and the man of fifty-one's finding in a doorway is not one of the three referents and his own chapter says so.** The cook has still not been told her own number. **Owner: the case.**

14. **THE PRINTED RULE IS NAMED AND RESTRICTED AND WAS NOT INVOKED AND WAS NOT NAMED IN ANY MOUTH ON ANY OF THESE TEN DAYS.** The word naming the public body is not in the printed rule and never has been. **Owner: the case. The rule is not withdrawn, not destroyed and is not to be repealed in this volume.**

15. **THE CONTINUITY OFFICE WROTE TWO INSTRUCTIONS AND CONCEDED ITS WHOLE POSITION IN ONE SENTENCE, AND NONE OF IT TURNED ANYTHING.** **A count of hours, a reason, a person at the foot; four districts and four buildings; a second instruction that lit one stairwell for four hours and cost a crew and about nine hours of somebody's evening and a man's sleep; three refusals of a date without a reason; and one sentence of about twenty-nine words, in a room of about nine people who did not want it, conceding that an hour on a sheet is not a person and that he has written it as if it were.** **It is right about the load and wrong about four buildings, and being right about the load is why the sentence is necessary, and being wrong about four buildings is why the sentence is not enough.** The office did not get a new wing, was not defeated and was not turned. **Owner: the case, and it is not converted by anybody.**

16. **THE RESERVED-WORD CLASS: CLOSED AS A MATTER OF FACT, AND THE INSTRUMENT IS STILL THE POINT.** The word that belongs to a bag and to a person carrying a torch occurs zero times across these ten files, in prose, in a load book, in a document and in a mouth, and **the zero is a fresh walk on the finished files and the walk was on the word and not on the shape of a sentence around it.** Neither of the two known ways of breaking this guardrail was used: no document reported the word's absence and no document sorted by it. **What is carried forward is not the word but the wider rule, and this movement widened it: no load book in these ten files reports the absence of any prohibited thing, because the panel and the marker were spent in this movement and a document that reports the absence of a thing that is present in the same batch has used it. The recording of a guardrail's holding belongs in a summary and not in a chapter.** Owner: every writer who opens a load book in Movement V.

17. **THE MAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT WITH A BAD HIP: SPENT, IN FULL, ACROSS FOUR OF THESE TEN DAYS, AND NOT THANKED ON ANY OF THEM.** He wrote the order, wrote the second instruction, refused three requests for a date, lit a stairwell at his own cost, said one sentence to a room that did not want it, and is right about the load and wrong about four buildings. **His count of two things said in about four years is still two; the sentence in Chapter 336 is not counted, the counting rule is Movement III's and it holds, and the count is not to be increased by a later pass. Nobody thanked him and he has said he will not be thanked and nobody is to.** **THE THIRD OF THE FIVE THINGS HE HAS BEEN ASKED ABOUT IS ONE HE REFUSED IN AN EARLIER TERM AND IT HAS NOT BEEN PUT TO HIM SINCE.** **Owner: him, and he is owed nothing, and nobody thanked him in any of the three volumes he has been in and nobody is to.**

18. **THE RING BINDER, THE PAGE, THE DRAWER, THE BOX, THE PLATE, THE CHAIR, THE FIELDS, THE LINE, AND EVERYTHING ELSE THAT DID NOT MOVE.** The woman's page: the eighth thing in a ring binder, unread, and nine people have decided not to read it. The master's fifth line: blank, and the drawer was not opened on any of these ten days. The pencil box in Bower Street: untouched, one name, no reference, on no system. The plate in a service cupboard: not explained, not removed, not blamed on anybody, and the man who found it is in none of these ten chapters. The ninth chair: against the wall, not moved back, mover not named. The licensor: unnamed, the field blank, the first year of that unit still free for a reason nobody has given. The nine keys: two exist, the first cannot be asked back, the second is in a pocket and has not been asked to go anywhere, and **the woman of thirty-one who holds it is in none of these ten chapters and was not asked and is not to be asked before Movement V.** The eleven buildings: still eleven, and the four-storey building in the third of the four districts where the night happened is not one of them. The four seconds of an earlier term: settled, not re-opened. The courier of twenty-one: in none of these ten chapters, still not forgiven the word, still not asked to sign anything, still not going to become a yes. The man of thirty-four who works nights: in none of these ten chapters, not named, not asked about, and on nothing. **Owner: each of these, unchanged, and none of them moved in this movement and the not moving is recorded so that a later writer can see which of them are load-bearing.**

19. **THE BAND SCORE IS THE BEST IN THE VOLUME ON THREE OF ITS FOUR FIGURES AND THE WORST ON ONE, AND BOTH ARE SAID HERE.** Word total 32,228. Bold spans 166 at 5.151, worst chapter 6.442 — **the lowest in the volume.** Overlap mean 68.8 — **below Movement II's 97.7 and Movement III's 104.9 and above Movement I's 50.0, and the per-paragraph instrument finds no apparatus paragraph at or above sixty shared shingles and a highest of forty.** Apparatus share 44.68 — **under Movement I's 44.75, which is the number to beat, by eleven hundredths of a point, and the margin is small, and three chapters sit above it at 333, 340 and 336.** Hedge 886 true at 27.492 per thousand — **the worst figure in the volume, not repaired, and the reason is the register: this movement's subject is a count of hours and a count of doors and about is the word a narrator uses in front of a quantity he has been given rather than measured.** **There is no ceiling on that figure anywhere in this repository and none was invented.** **Owner: the case, and none of these has to be closed inside a fortnight.**
